# CreoX J-Link API Reference - Asynchronous (pfcasync.jar)

Source: `D:\appli\Creo 3.0\M120\Common Files\text\java\pfcasync.jar`.

Generated with `javap -public`, directly against the real jar, one class at a time -- not hand-transcribed and not the byte-level parser the original dictionary used (this machine now has a JDK). Every method, every parameter type, every declared exception is exactly what the compiled class exposes. Fully-qualified `com.ptc.*` type names are shortened to their simple class name for readability (e.g. `Model` rather than `com.ptc.pfc.pfcModel.Model`); `java.lang`/`java.util`/`java.io` are shortened the same way. Nothing else is altered -- modifiers, parameter order, and `throws` clauses are verbatim.

**60 packages, 987 classes/interfaces, 6807 members.**
